# **Dispatching Problem Definition**

&nbsp;

## **1\. Unified Problem Classification**

The comprehensive fulfillment optimization problem is formally classified as the **Extended Rich Multi-Depot, Multi-Trip, Multi-Commodity Pickup-and-Delivery Problem with Open Time Windows, 3D Containerization, Human-Robot Shared Spaces, and Stochastic Disruption Recourse** ([![][image1]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BP%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BER-MD-VRPTW-3D-HRI%7D%7D#2)).

Below is the extended mathematical architecture, integrating the foundational operational structure with six additional real-world warehouse restrictions.

&nbsp;

### **Extended Notation, Sets, and Parameters**

#### **Extended Graph and Topological Sets**

* [![][image2]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%20%3D%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BASRS%7D%7D%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcharge%7D%7D#2): Extended vertex set.  
  * [![][image3]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BASRS%7D%7D%20%5Csubset%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P#2): Automated Storage and Retrieval System (AS/RS) induction spurs, vertical lift modules (VLMs), and mini-load crane pick faces.  
* [![][image4]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D%20%3D%20%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bauto%7D%7D%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmixed%7D%7D%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bhuman%7D%7D#2): Partition of physical floor zones into fully automated AMR zones, mixed cobot/human aisles, and pedestrian-only manual picking galleries.  
* [![][image5]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D#2): Discrete set of physical cargo items/SKUs, where each item [![][image6]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D#2) has a 3D bounding box dimension [![][image7]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bdim%7D_i%20%3D%20%5Bl_i%2C%20w_i%2C%20h_i%5D%5E%5Ctop#2), mass [![][image8]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=m_i#2), fragility rating [![][image9]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crho_i%20%5Cin%20\(0%2C%201%5D#2), and maximum allowable compressive load [![][image10]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csigma_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D#2).  
* [![][image11]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_k#2): Array of discrete onboard physical tote slots or roll-cage bays in vehicle [![][image12]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#2).

  #### **Additional Operational Parameters**

* [![][image13]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bv%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsafe%7D%7D\(%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D\)#2): Maximum permissible velocity vector inside zone [![][image14]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D#2), where [![][image15]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bv%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsafe%7D%7D\(%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmixed%7D%7D\)%20%5Cll%20%5Cmathbf%7Bv%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsafe%7D%7D\(%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bauto%7D%7D\)#2) pursuant to ISO 3691-4 industrial safety standards.  
* [![][image16]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=R_k%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(%5Ctheta\)#2): Dynamic polygonal footprint/swept swept-volume radius of vehicle [![][image17]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2) during rotational maneuvers at angular displacement [![][image18]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctheta#2).  
* [![][image19]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_c#2): Continuous processing/packing capacity rate of consolidation station [![][image20]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#2) (items per unit time).  
* [![][image21]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=t_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D\(%5Cell_1%2C%20%5Cell_2\)#2): Deterministic or scheduled transit duration of the AS/RS crane carriage between vertical coordinates [![][image22]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cell_1%2C%20%5Cell_2%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathbb%7BR%7D%5E3#2).  
* [![][image23]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstockout%7D%7D%20%5Cin%20%5B0%2C%201\)#2): Empirical probability of an inventory discrepancy/phantom stock event at pick face [![][image24]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P#2).  
* [![][image25]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=1%20-%20%5Cepsilon#2): Required probabilistic confidence level for on-time order delivery (chance constraint).

  ### 

  ## **2\. Constraints**

  ### 

  2.1. Spatial Topology and Routing Constraints

* **Directed Graph Traversability:** Routing is restricted to feasible directed arcs $(i, j) \\in \\mathcal{A}$; transitions across unlinked coordinates (e.g., across physical rack uprights) are strictly forbidden:  
  [![][image26]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cnotin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D#0)  
* **One-Way Aisle Flow:** Aisle directionality mandates unidirectional movement along specific graph subsets:  
  [![][image27]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bji%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bone-way%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Node Flow Conservation:** A picker or robot entering an intermediate pick or drop node must depart from it:  
  [![][image28]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%7D%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20-%20%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%7D%20x_%7Bji%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#0)  
* **Single Tour Continuity:** Every deployed vehicle must originate from an assigned start depot/charging berth and terminate at an assigned end depot/consolidation dock:  
  [![][image29]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%20%5Csetminus%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%7D%20x_%7Bd_s%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%201%2C%20%5Cquad%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%20%5Csetminus%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%7D%20x_%7Bi%2C%20d_e%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20d_s%2C%20d_e%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0#0)  
* **Subtour Elimination:** No disconnected cyclic routes isolated from the depot network may exist:  
  [![][image30]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BS%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BS%7D%7D%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BS%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20-%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20%5Cmathcal%7BS%7D%20%5Csubseteq%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BS%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%5Cge%202%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)

  2.2. Temporal and Scheduling Constraints

* **Time Propagation Along Arcs:** Arrival time at node $j$ must account for arrival time at node $i$, service/pick time $s\_i$, and kinematic travel duration $t\_{ij}^k$:  
  [![][image31]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_j%5Ek%20%5Cge%20T_i%5Ek%20%2B%20s_i%20%2B%20t_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20-%20M\(1%20-%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Precedence (Pickup Before Delivery):** The pick event for SKU line $i$ must strictly precede its delivery/drop-off at consolidation node $c(i)$:  
  [![][image32]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_i%5Ek%20%2B%20s_i%20%2B%20t_%7Bi%2C%20c\(i\)%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20T_%7Bc\(i\)%7D%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Order Completion Makespan Limit:** Maximum duration across all routes cannot exceed the global shift or dispatch cut-off $T\_{\\text{max}}$:  
  [![][image33]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_i%5Ek%20%5Cle%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Maximum Continuous Working Time / Shift Limits:** Continuous operational time per picker/vehicle without maintenance, dwell, or driver rest cannot exceed $H\_{\\text{shift}}$:  
  [![][image34]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_%7Bd_e%7D%5Ek%20-%20T_%7Bd_s%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20H_%7B%5Ctext%7Bshift%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)

  2.3. Open Windows Constraints

* **Asymmetric Lower-Bounded (Open-Upper) Pick Windows:** Available pick locations where replenishment or bin presentation occurs at $e\_i$, but no hard deadline truncates picking:  
  [![][image35]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_i%5Ek%20%5Cge%20e_i%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bopen-upper%7D%7D#0)  
  [![][image36]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=b_i%20%3D%20%5Cinfty#0)  
* **Asymmetric Upper-Bounded (Open-Lower) Drop Windows:** Consolidation chutes, dynamic buffers, or pack-station induction zones where arrival can happen arbitrarily early but must finish prior to container seal-off $L\_i$:  
  [![][image37]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_i%5Ek%20%5Cle%20L_i%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bopen-lower%7D%7D#0)  
  [![][image38]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=a_i%20%3D%20-%5Cinfty#0)  
* **Wait-State Non-Negativity:** When arriving prior to the lower boundary of an open window, idle dwell time $W\_i^k \\ge 0$ is permitted, holding vehicle release:  
  [![][image39]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_i%5Ek%20%3D%20%5Cmax\(e_i%2C%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Barrival%7D%7D%5Ek\)#0)  
* **Buffer Lateness Soft Thresholds (Piecewise Open Window Cost):** Departure beyond the target time $L\_i$ does not truncate the feasibility domain, but triggers unbounded linear or quadratic penalties:  
  [![][image40]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5CDelta_i%5Ek%20%5Cge%20T_i%5Ek%20-%20L_i%2C%20%5Cquad%20%5CDelta_i%5Ek%20%5Cge%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bopen-lower%7D%7D#0)

  2.4. Demand, Capacity, and Physical Payload Constraints

* **Multi-Dimensional Capacity Bounds:** Cumulative payload must not exceed vehicle limits across mass, volume, and tote slot limits at any node along the tour:  
  [![][image41]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_i%5Ek%20%3D%20%5Bu_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7Bmass%7D%7D%5Ek%2C%20u_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%5Ek%2C%20u_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7Bslots%7D%7D%5Ek%5D%5E%5Ctop%20%5Cle%20%5Cmathbf%7BQ%7D_k%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Dynamic Load Propagation (Picks):** Departing load increases upon processing a pick node $j \\in \\mathcal{V}\_P$:  
  [![][image42]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_j%5Ek%20%5Cge%20%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_i%5Ek%20%2B%20%5Cmathbf%7Bq%7D_j%20y_%7Bjo%7D%5Ek%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7BM%7D\(1%20-%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Dynamic Load Propagation (Deliveries):** Departing load decreases upon discharging inventory at consolidation node $c \\in \\mathcal{V}\_D$:  
  [![][image43]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_c%5Ek%20%5Cle%20%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_i%5Ek%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7Bq%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdrop%7D%7D%20%2B%20%5Cmathbf%7BM%7D\(1%20-%20x_%7Bic%7D%5Ek\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20c\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Full Demand Satisfaction:** Every item demand for order $o$ must be completely collected:  
  [![][image44]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20y_%7Bio%7D%5Ek%20%3D%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20o%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P\(o\)#0)  
* **Split Delivery/Pick Thresholds:** If split picking is forbidden for specific fragile or high-value SKUs:  
  [![][image45]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=y_%7Bio%7D%5Ek%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B0%2C%201%5C%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bnon-splittable%7D%7D#0)

  2.5. Kinematic, Spatial Separation, and Safety Constraints

* **Spatial Separation (Anti-Collision Margin):** Vehicles $k$ and $k'$ cannot occupy the same intersection or node $i$ simultaneously within safety clearance $\\delta\_{\\text{clearance}}$:  
  [![][image46]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvert%7B%7DT_i%5Ek%20-%20T_i%5E%7Bk'%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%5Cge%20%5Cdelta_%7B%5Ctext%7Bclearance%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cneq%20k'%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D#0)  
* **Headway on Single-Lane Segments:** Longitudinal trailing clearance must be maintained between successive vehicles along corridor segment $(i, j)$:  
  [![][image47]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_j%5E%7Bk'%7D%20-%20T_j%5Ek%20%5Cge%20%5CDelta%20t_%7B%5Ctext%7Bfollow%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bif%20%7D%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%3D%201%20%5Ctext%7B%20and%20%7D%20x_%7Bij%7D%5E%7Bk'%7D%20%3D%201%20%5Ctext%7B%20with%20%7D%20T_i%5E%7Bk'%7D%20%3E%20T_i%5Ek#0)  
* **Head-on Deadlock Elimination (Bilateral Traversal):** Bidirectional travel along narrow aisles cannot occur concurrently:  
  [![][image48]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%2B%20x_%7Bji%7D%5E%7Bk'%7D%20%5Cle%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bnarrow%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cneq%20k'%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bfor%20%7D%20%5BT_i%5Ek%2C%20T_j%5Ek%5D%20%5Ccap%20%5BT_j%5E%7Bk'%7D%2C%20T_i%5E%7Bk'%7D%5D%20%5Cneq%20%5Cemptyset#0)  
* **Turning Radius and Kinematic Acceleration:** Maximum turn rate and speed caps limit traversal duration based on cornering angle $\\theta\_{ij}$:  
  [![][image49]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=v_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20v_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D\(%5Ctheta_%7Bij%7D%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_i%5Ek\)#0)

  2.6. Energy, Battery, and Charging Constraints

* **State of Charge (SoC) Dynamics:** Battery consumption is a function of traversed distance, payload weight $\\mathbf{u}\_i^k$, and lifter actuation:  
  [![][image50]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BSoC%7D_j%5Ek%20%5Cle%20%5Ctext%7BSoC%7D_i%5Ek%20-%20%5Cleft\(%20%5Cepsilon_%7B%5Ctext%7Btare%7D%7D%20%2B%20%5Cbeta_%7B%5Ctext%7Bload%7D%7D%20u_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7Bmass%7D%7D%5Ek%20%5Cright\)%20d_%7Bij%7D%20%2B%20M\(1%20-%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek\)#0)  
* **Minimum Discharge Threshold:** Battery must not drop below critical operational reserves:  
  [![][image51]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BSoC%7D_i%5Ek%20%5Cge%20%5Ctext%7BSoC%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmin%7D%7D%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Charging Station Capacity:** Total concurrent vehicles at charging nodes $\\mathcal{V}\_{\\text{charge}} \\subset \\mathcal{V}\_0$ cannot exceed physical plug counts:  
  [![][image52]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bin%7D%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20t%20%5Cle%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bout%7D%7D%5Ek\)%20%5Cle%20C_%7B%5Ctext%7Bplugs%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20t#0)

  2.7. Warehouse Resource and Material Handling Constraints

* **Node Capacity (Aisle/Pick-Face Congestion):** Maximum number of active vehicles simultaneously inside aisle/zone $\\mathcal{Z}$:  
  [![][image53]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(T_i%5Ek%20%5Cle%20t%20%5Cle%20T_i%5Ek%20%2B%20s_i\)%20%5Cle%20C_%7B%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20t#0)  
* **Drop Station (Consolidation Chute) Capacity:** Total active batch containers assigned to pack station $c \\in \\mathcal{V}\_D$ cannot exceed physical put-wall apertures:  
  [![][image54]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bo%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(%5Ctext%7BOrder%20%7D%20o%20%5Ctext%7B%20routed%20to%20%7D%20c\)%20%5Cle%20B_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bapertures%7D%7D#0)  
* **Equipment-SKU Compatibility Matrix:** Heavy, refrigerated, or hazmat items can only be handled by certified pickers/specialized vehicle types:  
  [![][image55]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bif%20%7D%20%5Ctext%7BClass%7D\(j\)%20%5Cnotin%20%5Ctext%7BAllowedClasses%7D\(k\)#0)  
* **Ergonomic and Vertical Lift Boundaries:** Pick nodes located at high vertical elevations ($z \> h\_{\\text{reach}}$) require equipment outfitted with vertical mast lifters:  
  [![][image56]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=y_%7Bio%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bif%20%7D%20z_i%20%3E%20h_k%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmast%7D%7D#0)  
* **Hazmat and SKU Incompatibility:** Incompatible chemical classes cannot be transported concurrently within the same vehicle chassis:  
  [![][image57]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=u_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7BhazmatA%7D%7D%5Ek%20%5Ccdot%20u_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7BhazmatB%7D%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)

  2.8. Batching, Consolidation, and Synchronicity Constraints

* **Consolidation Synchronization:** Items belonging to multi-line order $o$ fulfilled by different vehicles $k \\ne k'$ must arrive at packing station $c$ within maximum staging shelf-life $\\tau\_{\\text{sync}}$ to prevent buffer clogging:  
  [![][image58]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvert%7B%7DT_c%5Ek\(o\)%20-%20T_c%5E%7Bk'%7D\(o\)%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%5Cle%20%5Ctau_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsync%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%2C%20k'%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D_o#0)  
* **Maximum Batch Size:** Total discrete orders grouped into a single multi-order pick route cannot exceed tote capacity:  
  [![][image59]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D_k%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%5Cle%20N_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%5C_orders%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)

  ### **2.9. Multi-Depot (MD) Network & Fleet Base Constraints**

  Let the depot set be partitioned into starting origins and terminating return locations:

  &nbsp;

  [![][image60]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%20%3D%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstart%7D%7D%20%5Ccup%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bend%7D%7D%20%3D%20%5C%7Bd_1%5Es%2C%20d_2%5Es%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20d_m%5Es%5C%7D%20%5Ccup%20%5C%7Bd_1%5Ee%2C%20d_2%5Ee%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20d_m%5Ee%5C%7D#0)

  Where each depot $d \\in \\mathcal{V}\_0$ represents a distinct charging bay, AMR staging hub, high-bay induction spur, or pick-cart depot across mezzanine levels or facility zones.

* **Depot Allocation and Assignment Invariance:** Every vehicle $k \\in \\mathcal{K}$ is assigned a base origin $d\_s(k) \\in \\mathcal{V}\_0^{\\text{start}}$. Vehicle $k$ cannot initiate trips from any unassigned base:  
  [![][image61]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%20%5Csetminus%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%7D%20x_%7Bd_s%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d_s%20%5Cneq%20d_s\(k\)%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Single-Depot Departure Activation:** A vehicle initiates at most one operational route from its designated base depot:  
  [![][image62]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%7D%20x_%7Bd_s\(k\)%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Depot Boundary Ingress/Egress Isolation:** Direct non-operational traversals between depots without visiting intermediate picking or dropping stations are blocked:  
  [![][image63]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bd%2C%20d'%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstart%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20d'%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bend%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Return Policy Topology:**  
  * **Fixed Return (Closed Multi-Depot):** The vehicle must conclude its tour at the exact physical base from which it departed:  
    [![][image64]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%7D%20x_%7Bi%2C%20d_e\(k\)%7D%5Ek%20%3D%20%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%7D%20x_%7Bd_s\(k\)%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bwhere%20%7D%20%5Ctext%7BBase%7D\(d_e\(k\)\)%20%3D%20%5Ctext%7BBase%7D\(d_s\(k\)\)#0)  
  * **Flexible/Open Return (Floating Fleet MD):** The vehicle may return to *any* depot within the network $\\mathcal{V}\_0^{\\text{end}}$ to optimize fleet redistribution and cross-dock rebalancing:  
    [![][image65]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bd_e%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bend%7D%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%7D%20x_%7Bi%2C%20d_e%7D%5Ek%20%3D%20%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%7D%20x_%7Bd_s\(k\)%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Depot Staging and Berthing Capacity Limits:** The total volume of vehicles departing from or returning to depot $d$ cannot exceed physical parking lanes, induction lifters, or floor dock bays:  
  [![][image66]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%7D%20x_%7Bd_s%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20C_%7Bd_s%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bout%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d_s%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstart%7D%7D#0)  
  [![][image67]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%7D%20x_%7Bi%2C%20d_e%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20C_%7Bd_e%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bin%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d_e%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bend%7D%7D#0)  
* **Inter-Depot Dynamic Fleet Rebalancing Balance:** Across a planning horizon, the net change in vehicle inventory at each physical depot $d$ must satisfy target floor reserves $\[\\underline{F}\_d, \\bar{F}\_d\]$:  
  [![][image68]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cunderline%7BF%7D_d%20%5Cle%20F_d%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Binitial%7D%7D%20-%20%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bj%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%7D%20x_%7Bd_s%2C%20j%7D%5Ek%20%2B%20%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%7D%20x_%7Bi%2C%20d_e%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20%5Cbar%7BF%7D_d%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B1%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20m%5C%7D#0)  
* **Multi-Depot SKU Inventory Availability Mapping:** A pick task $i \\in \\mathcal{V}\_P$ demanding SKU $s$ can only be routed from depot $d\_s(k)$ if the SKU is stocked within the reachable zone/aisle topology serviced by that depot:  
  [![][image69]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bd_s\(k\)%2C%20i%7D%5Ek%20%3D%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bif%20%7D%20%5Ctext%7BStock%7D\(s%2C%20%5Ctext%7BZone%7D\(d_s\(k\)\)\)%20%3D%200#0)  
* **Depot Temporal Operating Windows (Gate/Induction Cutoffs):** Dispatch operations at origin depots and intake processing at return docks are constrained by local operating shifts:  
  [![][image70]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=e_%7Bd_s%7D%20%5Cle%20T_%7Bd_s%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20l_%7Bd_s%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d_s%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstart%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
  [![][image71]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=e_%7Bd_e%7D%20%5Cle%20T_%7Bd_e%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20l_%7Bd_e%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20d_e%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_0%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bend%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)

  &nbsp;

  ### **Additional Formulation Restrictions**

  #### **Restriction 10: 3D Geometric Bin Packing, Stack Stability, and LIFO Retrieval**

  Payloads cannot be modeled merely as aggregate scalars of volume and mass; they require physical stability, load-bearing feasibility, and Last-In-First-Out (LIFO) extraction without intermediate re-handling.

* **Spatial Coordinate Allocation:** For each item [![][image72]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D#2) loaded onto vehicle [![][image73]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2), its spatial coordinates [![][image74]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=\(x_p%5Ek%2C%20y_p%5Ek%2C%20z_p%5Ek\)#2) within the onboard cargo envelope [![][image75]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5BL_k%2C%20W_k%2C%20H_k%5D#2) must satisfy non-overlapping boundaries:  
  [![][image76]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_p%5Ek%20%2B%20l_p%20%5Cle%20x_q%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Clor%20%5Cquad%20x_q%5Ek%20%2B%20l_q%20%5Cle%20x_p%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Clor%20%5Cquad%20y_p%5Ek%20%2B%20w_p%20%5Cle%20y_q%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Clor%20%5Cquad%20y_q%5Ek%20%2B%20w_q%20%5Cle%20y_p%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Clor%20%5Cquad%20z_p%5Ek%20%2B%20h_p%20%5Cle%20z_q%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Clor%20%5Cquad%20z_q%5Ek%20%2B%20h_q%20%5Cle%20z_p%5Ek#0)  
  [![][image77]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cforall%20p%20%5Cneq%20q%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D_k\(t\)#0)  
* **Static Vertical Gravity Support:** Any item [![][image78]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p#2) placed at [![][image79]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=z_p%5Ek%20%3E%200#2) must be physically supported by underlying items [![][image80]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=q#2) beneath its base surface:  
  [![][image81]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bq%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D_k%20%3A%20z_q%5Ek%20%2B%20h_q%20%3D%20z_p%5Ek%7D%20%5Ctext%7BAreaOverlap%7D\(p%2C%20q\)%20%5Cge%20%5Ckappa_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsupport%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20\(l_p%20%5Ccdot%20w_p\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bwhere%20%7D%20%5Ckappa_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsupport%7D%7D%20%5Cin%20%5B0.75%2C%201.0%5D#0)  
* **Compressive Crushability Threshold:** The cumulative vertical gravitational load exerted by items stacked above item [![][image82]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=q#2) cannot exceed its crush rating:  
  [![][image83]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bp%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D_k%20%3A%20z_p%5Ek%20%5Cge%20z_q%5Ek%20%2B%20h_q%7D%20m_p%20%5Ccdot%20g%20%5Ccdot%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(%5Ctext%7BBaseAbove%7D\(p%2C%20q\)\)%20%5Cle%20%5Csigma_q%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20q%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D_k#0)  
* **LIFO Extraction Feasibility (No In-Transit Restacking):** If pick item [![][image84]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p#2) is delivered at drop station [![][image85]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c_p#2) earlier than item [![][image86]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=q#2) at [![][image87]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c_q#2) ([![][image88]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_%7Bc_p%7D%5Ek%20%3C%20T_%7Bc_q%7D%5Ek#2)), item [![][image89]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p#2) must not be physically blocked along the access direction vector [![][image90]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvec%7Bd%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdoor%7D%7D#2):  
  [![][image91]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=z_p%5Ek%20%5Cge%20z_q%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Clor%20%5Cquad%20%5Cvec%7Bd%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdoor%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20\(%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_p%5Ek%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_q%5Ek\)%20%3E%200%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(p%2C%20q\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BI%7D_k%20%3A%20T_%7Bc_p%7D%5Ek%20%3C%20T_%7Bc_q%7D%5Ek#0)

  #### **Restriction 11: Shared Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) and Mixed-Zone Safety**

  When mobile equipment and human pickers co-occupy aisle infrastructure, kinematics must dynamically adjust to human presence.

* **Zone-Dependent Speed Throttling:** The traversal velocity [![][image92]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=v_%7Bij%7D%5Ek#2) of an AMR on arc [![][image93]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=\(i%2C%20j\)#2) intersecting zone [![][image94]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D#2) is bounded by dynamic safety limits:  
  [![][image95]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=v_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%20v_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsafe%7D%7D\(%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D\)%20-%20%5CDelta%20v_%7B%5Ctext%7Bhuman%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bhuman%7D%7D%20%5Cneq%20%5Cemptyset\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D_%7B%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D%7D#0)  
* **Aisle Exclusion Under Manual Operation:** An AMR cannot enter narrow aisle segment [![][image96]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=\(i%2C%20j\)%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BA%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bnarrow%7D%7D#2) if a human picker [![][image97]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=h%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BH%7D#2) is actively executing an order line in that aisle:  
  [![][image98]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%5Cle%201%20-%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(%5Ctext%7BHumanInAisle%7D\(i%2C%20j%2C%20t\)\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20t%20%5Cin%20%5BT_i%5Ek%2C%20T_j%5Ek%5D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BAMR%7D%7D#0)  
* **Dynamic Stopping Sight Distance (SSDS):** The spatial clearance gap between vehicle [![][image99]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2) and human [![][image100]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=h#2) must scale quadratically with current payload mass and speed:  
  [![][image101]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7Bdist%7D\(k%2C%20h%2C%20t\)%20%5Cge%20d_%7B%5Ctext%7Bbrake%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmin%7D%7D%20%2B%20%5Cfrac%7B\(v_%7Bij%7D%5Ek\)%5E2%7D%7B2%20%5Ccdot%20a_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdecel%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bemergency%7D%7D\(u_%7Bi%2C%5Ctext%7Bmass%7D%7D%5Ek\)%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20t#0)

  #### **Restriction 12: AS/RS Crane & Vertical Hoist Synchronous Coupling**

  In modern automated facilities, AMRs do not directly access high-bay locations; they interface with cranes, vertical lift modules (VLMs), or shuttle lifts at designated transfer spurs.

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

High-Bay AS/RS Racking (Vertical Axis Z)  
  ┌───┬───┬───┐  
  │   │ █ │   │ ◄── AS/RS Crane extracts tote at (X\_crane, Y\_crane, Z\_crane)  
  ├───┼───┼───┤  
  │   │ │ │   │  
  └───┴─┼─┴───┘  
        ▼  
   \[Induction Spur V\_ASRS\]  ◄── Two-Echelon Synchronization Interface  
        ▲  
  ──────┴──────  
  \[ AMR Fleet \]             ◄── Horizontal Routing on Graph G

* **Two-Echelon Crane-AMR Handshake:** An AMR visiting an AS/RS pick face [![][image102]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BASRS%7D%7D#2) cannot service the SKU until the AS/RS crane deposits the donor tote onto the transfer spur at time [![][image103]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bready%7D%7D\(i\)#2):  
  [![][image104]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_i%5Ek%20%5Cge%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bready%7D%7D\(i\)%20-%20M\(1%20-%20x_%7Bji%7D%5Ek\)%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BASRS%7D%7D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#0)  
* **Crane Motion Scheduling Equilibrium:**  
  [![][image105]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bready%7D%7D\(i\)%20%3D%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstart%7D%7D%20%2B%20t_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D\(%5Ctext%7BRestLoc%7D%2C%20%5Ctext%7BRackLoc%7D\(i\)\)%20%2B%20s_%7B%5Ctext%7Bextract%7D%7D#0)  
* **Induction Spur Buffer Saturation:** The number of completed donor totes residing on the transfer spur [![][image106]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i#2) waiting for AMR collection cannot exceed the spur physical gravity-roller capacity [![][image107]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=C_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bspur%7D%7D#2):  
  [![][image108]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum_%7Bo%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D_i%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcrane%7D%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bready%7D%7D\(i%2C%20o\)%20%5Cle%20t%20%5Cle%20%5Cmin_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20T_i%5Ek\(o\)\)%20%5Cle%20C_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bspur%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20t#0)

  #### **Restriction 13: Consolidation Station Load Leveling and Queue Stability**

  Downstream packing and consolidation chutes [![][image109]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#2) possess finite processing capacity [![][image110]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_c#2). Uncoordinated routing triggers upstream buffer overflow and AMR blocking.

* **Packing Station Queue Fluid Dynamics:** Let [![][image111]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=Q_c\(t\)#2) denote the instantaneous queue length (in volume or item count) at drop node [![][image112]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#2). The queue accumulates via AMR discharges and drains via human/automated packing:  
  [![][image113]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cfrac%7Bd%20Q_c\(t\)%7D%7Bdt%7D%20%3D%20%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%7D%20x_%7Bic%7D%5Ek%20%5Ccdot%20%5Cmathbf%7Bq%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdrop%7D%7D%5Ek%20%5Ccdot%20%5Cdelta\(t%20-%20T_c%5Ek\)%20-%20%5Cmu_c%20%5Ccdot%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(Q_c\(t\)%20%3E%200\)#0)  
* **Hard Buffer Overflow Constraint:**  
  [![][image114]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=Q_c\(t\)%20%5Cle%20Q_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%5C_buffer%7D%7D%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20t%20%5Cin%20%5B0%2C%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D%5D#0)  
* **Inter-Station Workload Leveling:** Total SKU volume directed to any packing station [![][image115]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#2) must remain within bounded variation around the mean throughput across the planning horizon:  
  [![][image116]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cleft%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%7D%20x_%7Bic%7D%5Ek%20u_%7Bi%2C%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%5Ek%20-%20%5Cfrac%7B1%7D%7B%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%5Cvert%7B%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bc'%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%7D%20x_%7Bic'%7D%5Ek%20u_%7Bi%2C%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%5Ek%20%5Cright%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%5Cle%20%5Csigma_%7B%5Ctext%7Bbalance%7D%7D#0)

  #### **Restriction 14: Swept-Envelope Kinematics and Turnaround Blockage**

  Vehicles carrying loads do not occupy dimensionless points. During turns at intersections or T-junctions, the physical chassis and overhang sweep an enlarged spatial area, creating transient corridor blockages.

* **Rotational Swept-Envelope Clearance:** At any intersection node [![][image117]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D#2) where vehicle [![][image118]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2) executes a direction change [![][image119]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctheta_%7Bhij%7D%20%3D%20%5Cangle%20\(%5Cvec%7Bhi%7D%2C%20%5Cvec%7Bij%7D\)%20%3E%200#2), the swept disk radius [![][image120]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=R_k%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(%5Ctheta_%7Bhij%7D%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7Bdim%7D_k%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_i%5Ek\)#2) defines an exclusion zone:  
  [![][image121]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5CVert%7B%7D%20%5Cmathbf%7Bcoord%7D_i%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_%7Bk'%7D\(t\)%20%5CVert%7B%7D_2%20%5Cge%20R_k%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(%5Ctheta_%7Bhij%7D\)%20-%20M\(1%20-%20x_%7Bhi%7D%5Ek%20x_%7Bij%7D%5Ek\)#0)  
  [![][image122]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cforall%20t%20%5Cin%20%5BT_i%5Ek%20-%20%5Ctau_%7B%5Ctext%7Bturn%7D%7D%2C%20T_i%5Ek%20%2B%20%5Ctau_%7B%5Ctext%7Bturn%7D%7D%5D%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20k'%20%5Cneq%20k#0)  
* **T-Junction Tail-Swing Collision Invariance:** If vehicle [![][image123]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2) rotates into an aisle, its tail-swing must not enter the swept corridor of the intersecting perpendicular aisle when occupied by vehicle [![][image124]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k'#2):  
  [![][image125]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(k%2C%20%5Ctheta%2C%20t\)%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(k'%2C%20%5Ctheta'%2C%20t\)%20%3D%20%5Cemptyset%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20k%20%5Cneq%20k'%2C%20%5C%3B%20%5Cforall%20t#0)

  #### **Restriction 15: Stochastic SKU Discrepancies and Chance-Constrained Schedulability**

  Inventory levels recorded in the Warehouse Management System (WMS) exhibit discrepancies (e.g., bin missing items, damaged packaging). Routing plans must guarantee SLA dispatch cutoffs probabilistically.

* **Chance-Constrained Order Completion Deadline:** An order [![][image126]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=o%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D#2) must arrive at consolidation chute [![][image127]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c\(o\)#2) prior to shipping carrier departure [![][image128]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=L_o#2) with probability at least [![][image129]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=1%20-%20%5Cepsilon#2):  
  [![][image130]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbb%7BP%7D%5Cleft\(%5Cmax_%7Bk%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D_o%7D%20%5C%7B%20T_%7Bc\(o\)%7D%5Ek%20%5C%7D%20%5Cle%20L_o%20%5Cright\)%20%5Cge%201%20-%20%5Cepsilon%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20o%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D#0)  
* **Recourse Under Stockout Events:** If pick location [![][image131]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P\(o\)#2) fails due to stockout (occurring with probability [![][image132]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstockout%7D%7D#2)), recourse variable [![][image133]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=z_%7Bij%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Balt%7D%7D%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B0%2C%201%5C%7D#2) dynamically diverts vehicle [![][image134]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2) to secondary inventory node [![][image135]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=j%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bbackup%7D%7D\(s\)#2):  
  [![][image136]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_c%5Ek%20%5Cleftarrow%20T_c%5Ek%20%2B%20%5Csum_%7Bi%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%7D%20p_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bstockout%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20%5Cleft\(%20t_%7Bi%2C%20%5Ctext%7Bbackup%7D\(i\)%7D%5Ek%20%2B%20s_%7B%5Ctext%7Bbackup%7D\(i\)%7D%20%5Cright\)#0)  
  The augmented stochastic arrival time must satisfy the open-window drop bounds:  
  [![][image137]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbb%7BE%7D%5BT_c%5Ek%5D%20%2B%20z_%7B1-%5Cepsilon%7D%20%5Csqrt%7B%5Cmathbb%7BVar%7D\(T_c%5Ek\)%7D%20%5Cle%20L_c%20%2B%20%5CDelta_c%5Ek%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#0)

  ### **Global Extended Formulation Architecture**

  The complete system combines Restrictions 1 through 15 into a single optimization statement:

  [![][image140]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cbegin%7Bcases%7D%20%5Ctext%7BRestrictions%20%7D%201%20-%202%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BTopology%20Flow%2C%20Subtour%20Elimination%2C%20Makespan%20%5C%26%20Shift%20Durations%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%203%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BAsymmetric%20Open%20Time%20Windows%20\(Pick%20%7D%20b_i%20%3D%20%5Cinfty%2C%20%5Ctext%7B%20Drop%20%7D%20a_c%20%3D%20-%5Cinfty%20%5Ctext%7B%20with%20penalty%20%7D%20%5CDelta_c%5Ek\)%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%204%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BMulti-Dimensional%20Payload%20Capacity%20Vector%20%7D%20%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_i%5Ek%20%5Cle%20%5Cmathbf%7BQ%7D_k%20%5Ctext%7B%20and%20Split%20Picking%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestrictions%20%7D%205%20-%206%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BAnti-Collision%20Clearance%2C%20Kinematic%20Following%2C%20SoC%20Dynamics%20%5C%26%20Dock%20Berths%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestrictions%20%7D%207%20-%208%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BZone%20Congestion%2C%20Equipment%20Compatibilities%2C%20Multi-Line%20Order%20Synchronicity%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%209%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BMulti-Depot%20Fixed%2FFloating%20Return%2C%20Fleet%20Rebalancing%20%5C%26%20Inter-Depot%20Invariance%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%2010%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7B3D%20Geometric%20Packing%2C%20LIFO%20Extraction%20%5C%26%20Static%20Load%20Compression%20Stability%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%2011%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BShared%20HRI%20Zones%2C%20ISO%203691-4%20Velocity%20Throttling%20%5C%26%20Manual%20Aisle%20Exclusion%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%2012%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BTwo-Echelon%20AS%2FRS%20Crane%20Carriage%20Induction%20Synchronicity%20%5C%26%20Spur%20Capacities%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%2013%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BConsolidation%20Queue%20Fluid%20Balance%20%7D%20%5Cfrac%7BdQ_c%7D%7Bdt%7D%20%5Cle%20Q_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D%20%5Ctext%7B%20and%20Workload%20Leveling%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%2014%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BSpatial%20Swept-Envelope%20Enclosures%20%7D%20R_k%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(%5Ctheta\)%20%5Ctext%7B%20and%20Turnaround%20Clearance%7D%20%5C%5C%20%5Ctext%7BRestriction%20%7D%2015%3A%20%26%20%5Ctext%7BChance-Constrained%20SLA%20Satisfaction%20%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BP%7D\(T_c%5Ek%20%5Cle%20L_c\)%20%5Cge%201%20-%20%5Cepsilon%20%5Ctext%7B%20Under%20Stockout%20Risk%7D%20%5Cend%7Bcases%7D#0)

  ### **Algorithmic Decomposition of the Extended Architecture**

  Solving the fully integrated formulation directly is intractable for industrial instances ([![][image141]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%3E%2010%5E3%2C%20%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%3E%2010%5E2#2)). The mathematical dependencies dictate a four-tier hierarchical decomposition:

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  
│ TIER 1: Master Order Batching, Depot Allocation & Chance-Constrained Clust. │  
│ Formulates: Mixed-Integer Master Problem (Assignment, MD-Rebalance, Leveling)│  
│ Resolves: Restrictions 8, 9, 13, 15                                         │  
└──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘  
                                       │ Fixed Batches & Depot Bounds  
                                       ▼  
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  
│ TIER 2: 3D Containerization & Pallet Loading Subproblem (LIFO Checking)     │  
│ Formulates: 3D-BPP with Precedence / Compression Constraints                │  
│ Resolves: Restrictions 4, 10                                                │  
└──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘  
                                       │ Verified Valid Load Configurations  
                                       ▼  
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  
│ TIER 3: Routing Engine under Open Windows (ALNS / ESPPRC)                    │  
│ Formulates: Rich VRP with Open Windows, Asymmetric Penalties & Subtours     │  
│ Resolves: Restrictions 1, 2, 3, 6, 7                                        │  
└──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘  
                                       │ Nominal Tour Waypoints & Open Times  
                                       ▼  
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  
│ TIER 4: Spatiotemporal Multi-Agent Execution & Kinematic Deconfliction      │  
│ Formulates: Time-Space Reservation / Conflict-Based Search (CBS) \+ Crane sync│  
│ Resolves: Restrictions 5, 11, 12, 14                                        │  
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

1. **Tier 1 (Master Batching and SLA Risk Leveling):** Partitions raw demand lines into vehicle-sized multi-order batches, determines target multi-depot departure/return vectors, enforces workload distribution across consolidation gates [![][image142]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D#2), and checks chance constraints [![][image143]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbb%7BP%7D\(T%20%5Cle%20L\)%20%5Cge%201%20-%20%5Cepsilon#2).  
2. **Tier 2 (3D Volumetric and LIFO Feasibility):** Evaluates whether candidate batches can be physically loaded into the vehicle chassis. A 3D packing heuristic computes center-of-gravity balance and verifies that pickup sequences do not create LIFO occlusions during downstream consolidation drop-offs. If infeasible, cuts are returned to Tier 1\.  
3. **Tier 3 (Open-Window Route Sequencing):** Solves the routing graph using Adaptive Large Neighborhood Search (ALNS) or Branch-Price-and-Cut over the relaxed graph. The asymmetric open-window functions allow routes to expand temporal profiles dynamically without triggering hard infeasibility.  
4. **Tier 4 (Kinematic Deconfliction and AS/RS Synchronization):** Projects routes onto the continuous physical manifold. A Spatiotemporal Conflict-Based Search (CBS) or Priority-Based Search (PBS) algorithm schedules crane retrieval handshakes, checks swept envelopes at intersections, and throttles velocities in human-occupied aisles to prevent physical gridlock.

   ### 

   ### 

   ### **Empirical Falsification and Critical Boundary Analysis**

   A rigorous mathematical model must define the empirical observation that would falsify its descriptive validity (*verification code: lmn*).

   #### **Extended Falsification Test for the 15-Restriction Model**

   **Falsifying Observation:** If in an operational automated fulfillment center, dynamically throttling vehicle speeds in shared zones ([![][image144]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BZ%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bmixed%7D%7D#2)) under HRI constraints (Restriction 11\) reduces system-wide order completion makespan relative to static unthrottled operations while keeping queue overflows [![][image145]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=Q_c\(t\)#2) strictly bounded below [![][image146]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=Q_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%5C_buffer%7D%7D#2), the hierarchical decomposition model is falsified.

* **Underlying Cause of Model Breakdown:** Classical traffic flow models assume speed reductions monotonically decrease system throughput.  
* **Physical Reality:** In high-density automated warehouses, unthrottled AMR speeds trigger burst arrivals at consolidation buffers and intersection deadlocks (Restrictions 13 and 14). This overloads buffer capacities and stalls upstream feeder aisles.  
* If velocity dampening in shared zones acts as an implicit pacing mechanism—smoothing arrival distributions [![][image147]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cfrac%7BdQ_c%7D%7Bdt%7D#2), eliminating stop-and-go kinematic hysteresis, and reducing buffer starvation—then the linear delay assumptions underpinning the routing cost matrix [![][image148]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c_%7Bij%7D%5Ek#2) and objective function are invalid representations of the underlying industrial physics.

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

## **3\. Algorithms**

### **Algorithmic Hierarchy and Multi-Tier Priority Architecture**

To resolve the 15 operational restrictions of the Extended Multi-Depot Rich VRP with Open Windows ([![][image149]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BP%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BER-MD-VRPTW-3D-HRI%7D%7D#2)), candidate algorithms are ranked by computational tractability, solution quality, and real-time execution feasibility.

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

&nbsp;

TIER 1: BATCHING & CHANCE-CONSTRAINED CLUSTERING \[R8, R9, R13, R15\]  
  ├── Rank 1 (Primary):    Distributionally Robust SAA \+ Fuzzy C-Means (FCM) Soft Decomposition  
  ├── Rank 2 (Heuristic):  Non-Dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm III (NSGA-III)  
  └── Rank 3 (Baseline):   Constrained K-Means++ with Spatio-Temporal Manhattan Metric  
                                │  
                                ▼  
TIER 2: 3D CONTAINERIZATION & LIFO EXTRACTION \[R4, R10\]  
  ├── Rank 1 (Primary):    CP-SAT (Lazy Clause Generation with 3D diffn Propagator)  
  ├── Rank 2 (Heuristic):  Dual-Chromosome Genetic Packing (Sequence \+ Orientation) with Extreme Points  
  └── Rank 3 (Baseline):   Bi-Level Deep Q-Network (DQN) with Action-Masked Heuristics  
                                │  
                                ▼  
TIER 3: ROUTE SEQUENCING UNDER ASYMMETRIC OPEN WINDOWS \[R1, R2, R3, R6, R7\]  
  ├── Rank 1 (Primary):    Hybrid Genetic Search with Advanced Diversity Control (HGS-ADC)  
  ├── Rank 2 (Heuristic):  Adaptive Large Neighborhood Search (ALNS) with Asymmetric Penalties  
  └── Rank 3 (Baseline):   Branch-Price-and-Cut (BPC) with Bi-Directional Pulse Pricing  
                                │  
                                ▼  
TIER 4: KINEMATIC DECONFLICTION & CONTINUOUS HRI \[R5, R11, R12, R14\]  
  ├── Rank 1 (Primary):    Priority-Based Search (PBS) over Continuous Safe-Interval Graphs (SIPP)  
  ├── Rank 2 (Heuristic):  Distributed Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (D-NMPC)  
  └── Rank 3 (Baseline):   Kinodynamic Conflict-Based Search with Continuous Time (CCBS-CL)

### **Comprehensive Algorithmic Priority Matrix**

#### **Mathematical Formulation: Spatio-Temporal Fuzzy C-Means (FCM)**

Standard clustering algorithms enforce binary partitioning ([![][image168]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B0%2C%201%5C%7D#2)), failing to model split deliveries and concurrent multi-depot staging. In contrast, FCM assigns a continuous membership degree [![][image169]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cin%20%5B0%2C%201%5D#2) representing the affinity of pick request [![][image170]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P#2) to vehicle batch/depot cluster [![][image171]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#2):

&nbsp;

[![][image172]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmin_%7B%5Cmathbf%7BU%7D%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7BV%7D%7D%20J_m%20%3D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%3D1%7D%5EN%20%5Csum_%7Bc%3D1%7D%5EC%20\(%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D\)%5Em%20%5Ccdot%20D_%7B%5Ctext%7BST%7D%7D%5E2\(x_i%2C%20v_c\)%20%2B%20%5Crho%20%5Csum_%7Bc%3D1%7D%5EC%20%5Cmax%5Cleft\(0%2C%20%5Csum_%7Bi%3D1%7D%5EN%20%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20q_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%20-%20Q_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%5Cright\)%5E2#0)

&nbsp;

[![][image173]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BSubject%20to%3A%20%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bc%3D1%7D%5EC%20%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%3D%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B1%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20N%5C%7D%2C%20%5Cquad%20%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cge%200#0)

Where:

* [![][image174]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=m%20%5Cin%20\(1%2C%20%5Cinfty\)#2) is the fuzzifier (typically [![][image175]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=m%20%3D%202.0#2)), controlling cluster overlap.  
* [![][image176]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=D_%7B%5Ctext%7BST%7D%7D\(x_i%2C%20v_c\)#2) is the composite Spatio-Temporal Warehouse Metric:  
  [![][image177]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=D_%7B%5Ctext%7BST%7D%7D\(x_i%2C%20v_c\)%20%3D%20%5CVert%7B%7D%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_i%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bspatial%7D%7D%5CVert%7B%7D_1%20%2B%20%5Comega_%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%20%5Cvert%7B%7De_i%20-%20v_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%2B%20%5Comega_%7B%5Ctext%7Bchute%7D%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(c\(i\)%20%5Cneq%20c\(v_c\)\)#0)  
* [![][image178]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_i%20%3D%20%5Bx_i%2C%20y_i%5D%5E%5Ctop#2) accounts for Manhattan rack travel.  
* [![][image179]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Comega_%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%20%5Cvert%7B%7De_i%20-%20v_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D#2) penalizes grouping items with asynchronous open pick windows.  
* [![][image180]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D#2) directly translates to the split-pick decision variable [![][image181]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=y_%7Bio%7D%5Ek#2) in the master formulation.

  #### **Algorithmic Comparison: Batching & Depot Allocation**

| Criterion | Rank 1: FCM \+ DR-SAA | Rank 2: NSGA-III | Rank 3: Constrained K-Means++ |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Handling of Split Picking (R4)** | Native: Continuous [![][image182]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cin%20%5B0%2C%201%5D#2) directly populates split fraction [![][image183]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=y_%7Bio%7D%5Ek#2). | Moderate: Requires specialized fractional gene representations. | Poor: Binary assignment; must split orders manually prior to clustering. |
| **Open Window Sensitivity (R3)** | High: Evaluates temporal centroid affinity via [![][image184]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvert%7B%7De_i%20-%20v_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D#2). | High: Direct objective function evaluation of open-window lateness. | Moderate: Euclidean/Manhattan projections omit asymmetric time dimensions. |
| **Multi-Depot Rebalancing (R9)** | Embedded via Lagrangian penalties on depot capacity limits. | Embedded via dedicated objective vectors for inter-depot balance. | Post-hoc reallocation needed; frequently violates depot buffer capacities. |
| **Stochastic Robustness (R15)** | High: Chance-constrained Wasserstein radius bounds stockout variance. | Moderate: Requires expensive Monte Carlo simulations across individuals. | Zero: Assumes deterministic inventory availability. |

#### **Mathematical Formulation: Dual-Chromosome Genetic Packing Algorithm**

To overcome the combinatorial complexity of 3D packing with LIFO extraction constraints, an evolutionary structure operates across two coupled chromosomes:

&nbsp;

[![][image185]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BC%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bindividual%7D%7D%20%3D%20%5Clangle%20%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Cpi%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bseq%7D%7D%2C%20%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Ctheta%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Borient%7D%7D%20%5Crangle#0)

* [![][image186]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Cpi%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bseq%7D%7D%20%3D%20\(%5Cpi_1%2C%20%5Cpi_2%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Cpi_n\)#2): Permutation chromosome defining the loading priority queue of SKUs.  
* [![][image187]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Ctheta%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Borient%7D%7D%20%3D%20\(%5Ctheta_1%2C%20%5Ctheta_2%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Ctheta_n\)#2): Orientation chromosome, where [![][image188]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctheta_i%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B0%2C%201%2C%202%2C%203%2C%204%2C%205%5C%7D#2) selects one of six spatial rotations of the bounding box [![][image189]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Bl_i%2C%20w_i%2C%20h_i%5D%5E%5Ctop#2).

  The decoding heuristic projects items into the vehicle cargo hull using the **Extreme Point (EP)** coordinate selection rule:

* Initialize candidate points [![][image190]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D%20%3D%20%5C%7B\(0%2C%200%2C%200\)%5C%7D#2).  
* For each item [![][image191]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cpi_i#2), select the lowest-coordinate extreme point [![][image192]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=e%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D#2) that satisfies:  
  * Volumetric boundary: [![][image193]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=e%20%2B%20%5Cmathbf%7Bdim%7D\(%5Cpi_i%2C%20%5Ctheta_i\)%20%5Cle%20%5BL_k%2C%20W_k%2C%20H_k%5D%5E%5Ctop#2).  
  * Static support: [![][image194]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum%20%5Ctext%7BAreaOverlap%7D%20%5Cge%20%5Ckappa_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsupport%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20%5Ctext%7BArea%7D\(%5Cpi_i\)#2).  
  * Compressive load limit: [![][image195]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum%20m_%7B%5Ctext%7Babove%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20g%20%5Cle%20%5Csigma_%7B%5Cpi_i%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D#2).  
  * LIFO extraction: No downstream item obstructs the door-access ray:  
    [![][image196]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BR%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Baccess%7D%7D\(%5Cpi_i\)%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_q%20%3D%20%5Cemptyset%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20q%20%3A%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdrop%7D%7D\(q\)%20%3E%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdrop%7D%7D\(%5Cpi_i\)#0)  
* Update [![][image197]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D#2) by generating up to six new extreme points along the facets of the newly placed bounding box and pruning dominated coordinates.

  #### **Algorithmic Comparison: 3D Bin Packing & Containerization**

| Criterion | Rank 1: CP-SAT (Global diffn) | Rank 2: Dual-Chromosome GA | Rank 3: Action-Masked DRL |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Mathematical Optimality** | Exact: Proves packing optimality or detects true physical infeasibility. | Heuristic: Approximates maximal volume utilization. | Heuristic: Policy gradient maximization over training distributions. |
| **LIFO Precedence Enforcement (R10)** | Strict: Precedence graph encoded directly via linear spatial inequalities. | High: Embedded in fitness function via heavy penalty multipliers. | Moderate: Requires state-masking to disable invalid extraction actions. |
| **Static Center-of-Mass (CoM)** | Exact: Quadratic/Conic constraints ensure stability under acceleration. | Moderate: CoM evaluated as an objective metric during EP placement. | Poor: Frequently produces unstable cantilevered configurations. |
| **Execution Latency** | High: 1.0–30.0 seconds per vehicle pallet. | Medium: 100–800 milliseconds per vehicle pallet. | Ultra-Low: 5–20 milliseconds per vehicle pallet. |

#### **Mathematical Formulation: HGS-ADC Open-Window Tour Evaluation**

Hybrid Genetic Search with Advanced Diversity Control (HGS-ADC) represents routes as order permutations without trip delimiters (giant tours):

&nbsp;

[![][image198]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BT%7D%20%3D%20\(%5Ctau_1%2C%20%5Ctau_2%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Ctau_N\)#0)

The giant tour is partitioned into optimal vehicle routes using an extended **Bellman-Ford Split Algorithm** adapted for asymmetric open windows:

* Let [![][image199]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=V\(j\)#2) be the optimal cost to service the prefix [![][image200]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=\(%5Ctau_1%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Ctau_j\)#2).  
* Arc [![][image201]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=\(i%2C%20j\)#2) represents a candidate route servicing subsequence [![][image202]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=\(%5Ctau_%7Bi%2B1%7D%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Ctau_j\)#2) assigned to vehicle [![][image203]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2):  
  [![][image204]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=V\(j\)%20%3D%20%5Cmin_%7B0%20%5Cle%20i%20%3C%20j%7D%20%5Cleft%5C%7B%20V\(i\)%20%2B%20c_%7Bij%7D%5Ek%20%2B%20%5Cbeta%20%5Csum_%7Bu%3Di%2B1%7D%5Ej%20%5CDelta_u%5Ek%20%2B%20%5Cphi%20%5Cmax%5Cleft\(0%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7Bu%7D_j%5Ek%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7BQ%7D_k%5Cright\)%20%5Cright%5C%7D#0)  
* Under Open Time Windows, arrival time at node [![][image205]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=u#2) propagates as:  
  [![][image206]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=T_u%5Ek%20%3D%20%5Cmax\(e_u%2C%20T_%7Bu-1%7D%5Ek%20%2B%20s_%7Bu-1%7D%20%2B%20t_%7Bu-1%2C%20u%7D%5Ek\)#0)  
* If [![][image207]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=u%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bopen-upper%7D%7D#2), [![][image208]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=b_u%20%3D%20%5Cinfty#2), so no upper bound violation occurs.  
* If [![][image209]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=u%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_D%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bopen-lower%7D%7D#2), the asymmetric delay penalty is:  
  [![][image210]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5CDelta_u%5Ek%20%3D%20%5Cmax\(0%2C%20T_u%5Ek%20-%20L_u\)#0)

  To maintain genetic diversity and avoid premature convergence on warehouse grid graphs, HGS-ADC uses a bi-objective selection fitness incorporating both solution quality and contribution to population diversity:

  &nbsp;

  [![][image211]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BFit%7D\(p\)%20%3D%20%5Ctext%7BRank%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bcost%7D%7D\(p\)%20%2B%20%5Cleft\(1%20-%20%5Cfrac%7B%5Ctext%7Biter%7D%7D%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%5C_iter%7D%7D%5Cright\)%20%5Ccdot%20%5Ctext%7BRank%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdiversity%7D%7D\(p\)#0)

  Where [![][image212]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BRank%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdiversity%7D%7D\(p\)#2) is measured via Hamming distance of arc sets between individual [![][image213]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=p#2) and the surviving population.

  #### **Algorithmic Comparison: Route Sequencing**

| Criterion | Rank 1: HGS-ADC (Memetic) | Rank 2: Asymmetric ALNS | Rank 3: Branch-Price-and-Cut |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Open Upper Windows ([![][image214]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=b_i%20%3D%20%5Cinfty#2))** | Excellent: Evaluates arbitrary route expansion via dynamic programming Split. | High: Asymmetric penalty operators allow flexible sequence exploration. | Poor: State dominance in ESPPRC degenerates; labels cannot be pruned. |
| **Battery SoC Propagation (R6)** | High: Non-linear discharge curves tracked during chromosome evaluation. | High: Reinsertion operators explicitly evaluate charging station detours. | Moderate: Adds an extra resource dimension, exponentially expanding label states. |
| **Search Space Traversal** | Global: Genetic crossover escapes local grid-routing traps. | Local: Dependent on Shaw destruction neighborhoods and regret logic. | Truncated: Relies on early termination in large-scale branch-and-bound trees. |
| **Multi-Depot Routing (R9)** | Native: Chromosomes map to multi-depot terminal indices. | Native: Vehicle start/end depots parameterized per route. | Highly complex: Requires pricing subproblems per depot pair. |

#### **Mathematical Formulation: Priority-Based Search (PBS) with Swept Volumes**

Unlike standard Conflict-Based Search (CBS), which branches on all pairwise spatio-temporal conflicts, PBS establishes a dynamic directed acyclic priority graph [![][image215]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BG%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bpriority%7D%7D%20%3D%20\(%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%2C%20%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D_%7B%5Cprec%7D\)#2):

* If an intersection conflict occurs between vehicle [![][image216]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k#2) and vehicle [![][image217]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=k'#2):  
  [![][image218]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(k%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7Bx%7D_k\(t\)\)%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(k'%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7Bx%7D_%7Bk'%7D\(t\)\)%20%5Cneq%20%5Cemptyset%20%5Cquad%20%5Ctext%7Bfor%20%7D%20t%20%5Cin%20%5Bt_%7B%5Ctext%7Bstart%7D%7D%2C%20t_%7B%5Ctext%7Bend%7D%7D%5D#0)  
* PBS branches into two search nodes:  
  [![][image219]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BBranch%201%3A%20%7D%20k%20%5Cprec%20k'%20%5Cquad%20\(%5Ctext%7Bvehicle%20%7D%20k%20%5Ctext%7B%20has%20priority%20over%20%7D%20k'\)#0)  
  [![][image220]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BBranch%202%3A%20%7D%20k'%20%5Cprec%20k%20%5Cquad%20\(%5Ctext%7Bvehicle%20%7D%20k'%20%5Ctext%7B%20has%20priority%20over%20%7D%20k\)#0)  
* The lower-priority vehicle replans its trajectory using **Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP)**, treating the higher-priority vehicle's continuous swept hull as a dynamic spatial obstacle:  
  [![][image221]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BO%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdynamic%7D%7D\(t\)%20%3D%20%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_k\(t\)%20%5Coplus%20%5Cmathcal%7BP%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bchassis%7D%7D%20%5Coplus%20%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D\(R_k%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bsweep%7D%7D\(%5Ctheta_k\(t\)\)\)#0)

  #### **Algorithmic Comparison: Kinematic Deconfliction**

| Criterion | Rank 1: PBS \+ SIPP | Rank 2: Distributed NMPC | Rank 3: Kinodynamic CCBS-CL |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Scalability ([![][image222]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cvert%7B%7D%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%3E%20200#2))** | High: Empirical polynomial runtime; low replanning latency. | High: Fully distributed computation on onboard robot hardware. | Low: Combinatorial explosion of conflict branches at high densities. |
| **Human-Robot Safety (R11)** | Moderate: Requires treating humans as dynamic moving obstacles with speed buffers. | High: Native constraint handling for ISO 3691-4 deceleration profiles. | Low: Fixed spatio-temporal search spaces struggle with unpredictable human drift. |
| **AS/RS Crane Coupling (R12)** | Strict: Handshake times modeled as fixed temporal reservation intervals. | Moderate: Requires state tracking of the external crane carriage. | Strict: Joint optimization over both crane and AMR trajectory trees. |
| **Deadlock Elimination** | Complete: Acyclic priority structure prevents cyclic head-on blocking. | Incomplete: May get trapped in symmetric non-convex deadlocks without a recovery planner. | Complete: Guarantees complete resolution if a kinematically feasible path exists. |

### **Empirical Falsification and Critical Boundary Analysis**

Every rigorous optimization architecture must define the exact conditions under which its descriptive validity breaks down (*verification code: lmn*).

#### **Algorithmic Falsification Protocol**

**Falsifying Observation:** If in an automated fulfillment warehouse running at high order line densities, deploying a **Rank-1 Genetic / Soft-Clustering Pipeline** (FCM [![][image223]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crightarrow#2) 3D CP-SAT [![][image224]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crightarrow#2) HGS-ADC [![][image225]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crightarrow#2) PBS) results in higher total order cycle times and higher frequency of replanning events than a **Rank-3 Static Priority Framework** (Constrained K-Means++ [![][image226]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crightarrow#2) DRL Packing [![][image227]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crightarrow#2) Pulse BPC [![][image228]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Crightarrow#2) CCBS), the proposed tiered algorithmic hierarchy is falsified.

* **Failure Mechanism:** Soft-clustering (FCM) and genetic metaheuristics maximize solution diversity by exploiting wide solution neighborhoods. However, soft-clustering generates overlapping vehicle paths that intersect repeatedly across common corridors.  
* **Physical Gridlock:** Under high facility utilization ([![][image229]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%3E%2085%5C%25#2)), the computational cost of resolving spatiotemporal conflicts in Tier 4 (PBS) grows non-linearly with route overlap. If the upstream "better" metaheuristic routes from Tiers 1 and 3 create aisle congestion that overwhelms low-level deconfliction, the decoupled tiered hierarchy suffers from downstream infeasibility cascades.  
* In this regime, simple orthogonal partitioning (K-Means++ with strict non-overlapping aisle corridors) completely eliminates Tier 4 conflict-checking overhead, rendering the simpler model empirically superior in physical throughput.

  ### 

  ### **Systemic Recommendation**

  For large-scale order fulfillment operations processing more than 5,000 order lines per hour across mixed autonomous fleet environments:

* **Adopt the Tier 1 Rank-1 (FCM \+ SAA) Engine:** Utilize soft clustering to preserve split-pick flexibility and buffer destination leveling without prematurely fixing hard vehicle assignments.  
* **Decouple 3D Packaging into Pre-Computed Static Pallet Patterns:** Run Tier 2 CP-SAT asynchronously on top-moving SKU combinations to generate a cached lookup table of stable 3D bounding configurations, preventing runtime latency spikes.  
* **Execute Tier 3 via HGS-ADC:** Leverage memetic algorithms with asymmetric split operators to navigate open-ended time boundaries ([![][image230]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=b_i%20%3D%20%5Cinfty#2)) where exact Branch-Price-and-Cut pricing degenerates.  
* **Enforce Tier 4 PBS Deconfliction over SIPP:** Implement Priority-Based Search with dynamic continuous swept hulls, reserving distributed NMPC solely as an onboard reactive layer for ISO 3691-4 human safety compliance.

  ### **Algorithmic Hierarchy and Multi-Tier Priority Architecture**

  To resolve the 15 operational restrictions of the Extended Multi-Depot Rich VRP with Open Windows ([![][image149]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BP%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7BER-MD-VRPTW-3D-HRI%7D%7D#2)), candidate algorithms are ranked by computational tractability, solution quality, and real-time execution feasibility.

  ├── Rank 2 (Heuristic):  Distributed Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (D-NMPC)  
  └── Rank 3 (Baseline):   Kinodynamic Conflict-Based Search with Continuous Time (CCBS-CL)

#### **Mathematical Formulation: Spatio-Temporal Fuzzy C-Means (FCM)**

Standard clustering algorithms enforce binary partitioning ([![][image168]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B0%2C%201%5C%7D#2)), failing to model split deliveries and concurrent multi-depot staging. In contrast, FCM assigns a continuous membership degree [![][image169]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cin%20%5B0%2C%201%5D#2) representing the affinity of pick request [![][image170]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=i%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BV%7D_P#2) to vehicle batch/depot cluster [![][image171]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=c%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BK%7D#2):

&nbsp;

[![][image172]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmin_%7B%5Cmathbf%7BU%7D%2C%20%5Cmathbf%7BV%7D%7D%20J_m%20%3D%20%5Csum_%7Bi%3D1%7D%5EN%20%5Csum_%7Bc%3D1%7D%5EC%20\(%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D\)%5Em%20%5Ccdot%20D_%7B%5Ctext%7BST%7D%7D%5E2\(x_i%2C%20v_c\)%20%2B%20%5Crho%20%5Csum_%7Bc%3D1%7D%5EC%20%5Cmax%5Cleft\(0%2C%20%5Csum_%7Bi%3D1%7D%5EN%20%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20q_i%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%20-%20Q_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bvol%7D%7D%5Cright\)%5E2#0)

&nbsp;

[![][image173]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctext%7BSubject%20to%3A%20%7D%20%5Csum_%7Bc%3D1%7D%5EC%20%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%3D%201%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20i%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B1%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20N%5C%7D%2C%20%5Cquad%20%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D%20%5Cge%200#0)

Where:

* [![][image176]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=D_%7B%5Ctext%7BST%7D%7D\(x_i%2C%20v_c\)#2) is the composite Spatio-Temporal Warehouse Metric:  
  [![][image177]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=D_%7B%5Ctext%7BST%7D%7D\(x_i%2C%20v_c\)%20%3D%20%5CVert%7B%7D%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_i%20-%20%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bspatial%7D%7D%5CVert%7B%7D_1%20%2B%20%5Comega_%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%20%5Cvert%7B%7De_i%20-%20v_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D%20%2B%20%5Comega_%7B%5Ctext%7Bchute%7D%7D%20%5Cmathbb%7BI%7D\(c\(i\)%20%5Cneq%20c\(v_c\)\)#0)  
* [![][image178]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathbf%7Bpos%7D_i%20%3D%20%5Bx_i%2C%20y_i%5D%5E%5Ctop#2) accounts for Manhattan rack travel.  
* [![][image179]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Comega_%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%20%5Cvert%7B%7De_i%20-%20v_c%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Btime%7D%7D%5Cvert%7B%7D#2) penalizes grouping items with asynchronous open pick windows.  
* [![][image180]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmu_%7Bic%7D#2) directly translates to the split-pick decision variable [![][image181]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=y_%7Bio%7D%5Ek#2) in the master formulation.

#### **Mathematical Formulation: Dual-Chromosome Genetic Packing Algorithm**

To overcome the combinatorial complexity of 3D packing with LIFO extraction constraints, an evolutionary structure operates across two coupled chromosomes:

&nbsp;

[![][image185]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BC%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bindividual%7D%7D%20%3D%20%5Clangle%20%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Cpi%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bseq%7D%7D%2C%20%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Ctheta%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Borient%7D%7D%20%5Crangle#0)

* [![][image186]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Cpi%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Bseq%7D%7D%20%3D%20\(%5Cpi_1%2C%20%5Cpi_2%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Cpi_n\)#2): Permutation chromosome defining the loading priority queue of SKUs.  
* [![][image187]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cboldsymbol%7B%5Ctheta%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Borient%7D%7D%20%3D%20\(%5Ctheta_1%2C%20%5Ctheta_2%2C%20%5Cdots%2C%20%5Ctheta_n\)#2): Orientation chromosome, where [![][image188]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Ctheta_i%20%5Cin%20%5C%7B0%2C%201%2C%202%2C%203%2C%204%2C%205%5C%7D#2) selects one of six spatial rotations of the bounding box [![][image189]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Bl_i%2C%20w_i%2C%20h_i%5D%5E%5Ctop#2).

  The decoding heuristic projects items into the vehicle cargo hull using the **Extreme Point (EP)** coordinate selection rule:

* Initialize candidate points [![][image190]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D%20%3D%20%5C%7B\(0%2C%200%2C%200\)%5C%7D#2).  
* For each item [![][image191]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cpi_i#2), select the lowest-coordinate extreme point [![][image192]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=e%20%5Cin%20%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D#2) that satisfies:  
  * Volumetric boundary: [![][image193]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=e%20%2B%20%5Cmathbf%7Bdim%7D\(%5Cpi_i%2C%20%5Ctheta_i\)%20%5Cle%20%5BL_k%2C%20W_k%2C%20H_k%5D%5E%5Ctop#2).  
  * Static support: [![][image194]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum%20%5Ctext%7BAreaOverlap%7D%20%5Cge%20%5Ckappa_%7B%5Ctext%7Bsupport%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20%5Ctext%7BArea%7D\(%5Cpi_i\)#2).  
  * Compressive load limit: [![][image195]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Csum%20m_%7B%5Ctext%7Babove%7D%7D%20%5Ccdot%20g%20%5Cle%20%5Csigma_%7B%5Cpi_i%7D%5E%7B%5Ctext%7Bmax%7D%7D#2).  
  * LIFO extraction: No downstream item obstructs the door-access ray:  
    [![][image196]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BR%7D_%7B%5Ctext%7Baccess%7D%7D\(%5Cpi_i\)%20%5Ccap%20%5Cmathcal%7BB%7D_q%20%3D%20%5Cemptyset%20%5Cquad%20%5Cforall%20q%20%3A%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdrop%7D%7D\(q\)%20%3E%20T_%7B%5Ctext%7Bdrop%7D%7D\(%5Cpi_i\)#0)  
* Update [![][image197]](https://saxarona.github.io/mathjax-viewer/?input=%5Cmathcal%7BE%7D#2) by generating up to six new extreme points along the facets of the newly placed bounding box and pruning dominated coordinates.

  #### **Algorithmic Comparison: 3D Bin Packing & Containerization**

| Criterion | Rank 1: CP-SAT (Global diffn) | Rank 2: Dual-Chromosome GA | Rank 3: Action-Masked DRL |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Mathematical Optimality** | Exact: Proves packing optimality or detects true physical infeasibility. | Heuristic: Approximates maximal volume utilization. | Heuristic: Policy gradient maximization over training distributions. |
| **LIFO Precedence Enforcement (R10)** | Strict: Precedence graph encoded directly via linear spatial inequalities. | High: Embedded in fitness function via heavy penalty multipliers. | Moderate: Requires state-masking to disable invalid extraction actions. |
| **Static Center-of-Mass (CoM)** | Exact: Quadratic/Conic constraints ensure stability under acceleration. | Moderate: CoM evaluated as an objective metric during EP placement. | Poor: Frequently produces unstable cantilevered configurations. |
| **Execution Latency** | High: 1.0–30.0 seconds per vehicle pallet. | Medium: 100–800 milliseconds per vehicle pallet. | Ultra-Low: 5–20 milliseconds per vehicle pallet. |

  ### **Tier 3: Route Sequencing Under Asymmetric Open Time Windows**
